## Twenty-first pass: corrections-doc batch (8 items across HR, Hiring Manager, Management)

User uploaded a `Corrections.docx` with a flat list of UI/bug notes (some with screenshots) spanning HR Vacancies/Interviews, Hiring Manager, and Management. Extracted via `pandoc` + unzipping the docx for its 3 embedded screenshots (viewed directly to identify the exact screens referenced, since the text alone was ambiguous on a couple of items).

- **HR Interviews day-popup (the "+" menu with Assign Interview Panel / Schedule Interview / Add Candidate(s)) had no visible close affordance.** Clicking the backdrop already dismissed it, but that wasn't obvious from the menu alone. Added an explicit `&#10005;` close button (`ivw-plus-menu-close`, reusing the existing `.ivw-modal-close` styling), and gave `.ivw-plus-menu` `position: relative` + top padding so the absolutely-positioned X lands inside the small card instead of the full-screen backdrop.
- **Edit Vacancy panel still showed the Department dropdown** after it was removed from Create Vacancy (Sprint-era correction). Removed it from Edit too -- `form.department` is still set under the hood from `openEditForm`, so validation/save are unaffected, there's just no UI to change it (a vacancy's department shouldn't change post-creation).
- **The "&larr; Departments" back link (HR Vacancies, department drill-down) was small, easy to miss.** Bumped `.vac-back` from 14.5px/500 weight to 17px/600 weight, matching the "make back buttons bigger sitewide" precedent from earlier in the project.
- **Candidate Comparison's "Top Candidate Score Ranking" only ever showed 2 candidates, not 5.** `TOP_N` in `hiringManager.controller.ts` was already 5 and the frontend renders every row it's given -- not a code bug. The real cause: `comparisonBoost` (which adds 4 extra scored candidates) was only set on 2 of the 16 seeded vacancies (Marketing, IT), so every other department's flagship vacancy only had the 1-2 candidates that naturally stay `SHORTLISTED`-with-feedback out of the base archetypes. Added `comparisonBoost: true` to one vacancy per department in `seed-full-demo.ts` (HR Business Partner, Senior Financial Analyst, Operations Manager, Account Executive, Customer Success Manager, Corporate Counsel, on top of the existing Content Marketing Specialist and Backend Engineer) so every department has at least one vacancy that genuinely ranks 5.
- **"Hiring Manager doesn't have any interviews" -- root-caused, not just patched.** `listMyInterviews` is scoped strictly by `InterviewPanelist` membership. `hm.id` (the seeded Hiring Manager account) was only ever added as a panelist on a final round when `mgmt` was truthy, and `mgmt` is only non-null for IT (`mgmtForThisVacancy` in `main()`) -- so the HM was a panelist on exactly 2 of 16 vacancies. There's no actual business rule requiring an HM to attend any interview (checked `interview.controller.ts` -- `checkFinalRoundHasManagement` only cares about the `MANAGEMENT` role); this was purely a seed-data gap. `hm.id` is now added to every multi-stage vacancy's final-round panel unconditionally, so My Interviews has real entries across all 8 departments after a reseed.
- **"Can't see the Proceed option" -- investigated, not reproduced.** Read through the full chain (`VacancyCandidatesPage` -> `CandidateDecisionPage`, and `PendingDecisionsPage`, both frontend and their backend `isFinalRound`/`awaitingDecision` logic in `hiringManager.controller.ts`). Proceed/Do Not Proceed only renders when `awaitingDecision` is true and `isFinalRound` is false -- correct and consistent everywhere it's used; single-stage vacancies and genuinely-final rounds correctly show Hire/Reject instead, which is by design, not a bug. Could not find a code path that would hide Proceed for a real non-final, ready-for-decision candidate. Left open -- flagged to the user to ask for a screenshot or the exact candidate/vacancy next time this comes up, rather than guessing at a fix for a bug that couldn't be located.
- **HM Decision History: added candidate-profile viewing, deliberately did NOT add Proceed/Do Not Proceed.** Rows now navigate to the existing `CandidateDecisionPage`, which already renders correctly read-only for a decided application (shows the "already hired/rejected" banner or historical round, plus full feedback history, no action buttons since `awaitingDecision` is false). Chose not to add decision actions to this page: letting someone re-decide an already-made, logged decision from a history view (with no undo) would undermine the point of keeping a decision log -- if a decision needs revisiting, that should go through Pending Decisions' normal flow.
- **Combined Management's "My Candidates" and "Candidate Progress" into one page.** These were two different datasets (My Candidates = interviews where Management is personally a panelist and owes feedback; Candidate Progress = a read-only, department-wide oversight table), so this merges them as two sections on one page rather than picking one. Kept the route at `/management/candidate-progress` and the `.cp-section-title` selector specifically because `automated-tests/selenium/sprint2/test_management_reports.py` navigates there and asserts on that selector -- `/management/candidates` now redirects to it instead of being removed outright. Old `MyCandidatesPage.tsx` left in place with a "SUPERSEDED" header comment rather than deleted, matching this project's existing convention for retired seed scripts.

**Verification:** frontend `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` run directly in this sandbox and confirmed clean (exit 0) -- this sandbox's frontend TypeScript install is usable, unlike the backend's (confirmed via a deliberate-error sanity check: `tsc` correctly flagged a planted type error elsewhere, so the clean result on the real project is meaningful, not a silently-broken binary). Backend (`seed-full-demo.ts`) changes hand-reviewed only -- backend `tsc`/`esbuild` still unusable in this sandbox (Windows-native binaries mounted, same pre-existing issue). User needs to rerun `npx tsx scripts/seed-full-demo.ts` from `backend/` (idempotent, no need to re-wipe first) to pick up the comparisonBoost and HM-panelist seed changes.

## Twenty-second pass: follow-up screenshots on the same corrections batch

User sent 5 screenshots after the twenty-first pass: the same Top Candidate Score Ranking still showing only 2 (Benedict Okoro/Odette Moreau -- same names as before), two "Panel Feedback" pages for already-hired candidates with a lot of empty space on the right, the My Interviews calendar with the same empty-right-side issue, and a still-in-progress candidate's feedback page. Also restated the Proceed/Do Not Proceed complaint more specifically: "hire and reject is only for the final interview, the other mid interviews should be either proceed or do not proceed."

- **Top 5 still showing 2:** expected -- this is the exact same Benedict Okoro/Odette Moreau screenshot from the corrections doc, meaning `seed-full-demo.ts` hasn't been rerun since the Twenty-first pass's `comparisonBoost` change yet. No code change needed here; told the user to rerun it.
- **`CandidateDecisionPage` ("Panel Feedback - X") was hard-capped at `max-width: 720px`** -- a real, confirmed bug, unlike the calendar (below). Every other HM content page (`PendingDecisionsPage`, `VacancyCandidatesPage`) uses `max-width: 90vw`; this one page was narrower for no apparent reason, leaving the large empty gap the screenshots show. Fixed to match.
- **My Interviews calendar (no day selected) also left empty space on the right.** Read through the full flex chain (`.hm-main { flex: 1 }` -> `.hmi-page { width: 100% }` -> `.hmi-body { display: flex }` -> `.hmi-calendar-wrap { flex: 1 1 auto }`) and couldn't find an actual bug -- `flex-grow: 1` as the sole flex child should already fill the row. Added an explicit `width: 100%` to `.hmi-calendar-wrap` as a defensive belt-and-braces fix in case something about `flex-basis: auto` was under-sizing it in practice; low-risk either way.
- **"Only Hire/Reject, never Proceed/Do Not Proceed" -- re-investigated a second time, still couldn't reproduce or find a bug.** Re-read `getPendingDecisionsForHm`'s `isFinalRound` resolution (compares each row's round order against the vacancy's max round order, computed from a fresh `vacancyStage` query -- keyed correctly, no property mismatch), `CandidateDecisionPage.tsx`'s render gate (`data.awaitingDecision && !data.isFinalRound` for Proceed/Do Not Proceed), and the frontend/backend field names (`isFinalRound` matches exactly, no serialization mismatch). All 5 screenshots turned out to be either an already-hired read-only view (no action buttons at all, not evidence of the bug) or a candidate who had already walked every round including the final one (correctly final, correctly Hire/Reject). Working theory, not confirmed: the user has likely already actioned every round-1 "Proceed-eligible" candidate during earlier testing sessions, so what's left in Pending Decisions right now skews toward final-round items -- `seed-full-demo.ts` alone won't undo an already-advanced application (it's find-or-create), so only a full wipe (`wipe-candidates.ts` -> `prisma db seed` -> `seed-full-demo.ts`) would produce a guaranteed-fresh non-final example to check against. Told the user to do the full reset and check Pending Decisions immediately after, before actioning anything, and to send the specific candidate/vacancy if Proceed still never appears -- did not want to guess at a fix for a bug that couldn't be located twice.

**Verification:** frontend `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` re-run after these edits, still clean (exit 0). No backend changes this pass.

## Twenty-third pass: HM "My Interviews" removed (design reversal); comparison boost widened to all vacancies; close-X alignment fixed

- **Reversed the Twenty-first pass's HM-panelist seed fix, per explicit user correction.** That earlier pass added `hm.id` to every multi-stage vacancy's final-round panel to fix "Hiring Manager doesn't have any interviews." User came back and clarified the actual product decision instead: the HM isn't an interviewer -- their job is deciding Proceed/Do Not Proceed/Hire/Reject from Pending Decisions based on *other* people's feedback, not sitting on panels themselves. So the right fix wasn't more seed data, it was removing the feature: "My Interviews" nav item and both its routes (`/hiring-manager/my-interviews`, `/hiring-manager/my-interviews/:id/feedback`) removed from `HMLayout.tsx`/`App.tsx`; `hm.id` reverted back out of `seed-full-demo.ts`'s final-round panel entirely (not just re-gated behind `mgmt` -- fully removed, since it no longer belongs there at all). `pages/hiringManager/MyInterviewsPage.tsx` marked SUPERSEDED rather than deleted, matching this project's convention. Also cleaned up `interviewer/FeedbackPage.tsx` (shared by Interviewer/Management/HM), which still had an `isHiringManager` branch computing a `backTo`/`backLabel` for a route that no longer exists anywhere -- removed, now just Management vs. Interviewer.
- **`CandidateDecisionPage`'s Panel Feedback pages and the My Interviews calendar still looked empty on the right** in the user's screenshots even after the Twenty-second pass's `max-width: 90vw` fix and defensive `width: 100%` -- both were already applied and correct; no further layout change made here (the calendar issue is now moot since the page itself is gone).
- **Candidate Comparison "still only showing 2"**, again. Root cause this time: the Twenty-first pass's `comparisonBoost` fix only covered one vacancy per department (8 of 16) specifically to conserve seed-script size, which meant it depended on the user happening to open the *right* vacancy -- easy to hit the wrong one and see the old count. Removed that dependency entirely: `comparisonBoost: true` is now on all 15 non-`allDecided` vacancies (only `Support Team Lead` is deliberately excluded -- `allDecided` vacancies return out of `seedVacancyPipeline` before ever reaching the comparisonBoost block, since every candidate is already HIRED/REJECTED and none stay `SHORTLISTED`, so boosting it would be a silent no-op). Every open vacancy's Candidate Comparison page now genuinely has 5+ scored candidates, regardless of which one is opened.
- **Fixed a real, separate CSS bug: the `+` menu's new close-X (Twenty-first pass) had its hover highlight visibly offset from the X glyph.** `.ivw-modal-close` (shared by every modal close button in `InterviewsPage.tsx`, not just the new one) never had flex-centering -- a bare `<button>` doesn't center its own content, and the browser's default button padding was pushing the glyph off-center within the fixed 28x28 circular background. Added `display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; padding: 0;` -- fixes every close button on this page, not just the one that was screenshotted.

**Verification:** frontend `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` re-run after all edits, clean (exit 0). Backend (`seed-full-demo.ts`) hand-reviewed only -- same pre-existing sandbox limitation (backend `tsc`/`esbuild` unusable, Windows-native binaries). User needs to rerun `npx tsx scripts/seed-full-demo.ts` from `backend/` to pick up the comparisonBoost widening; the hm.id panelist reversal has no visible effect either way since the page reading it is gone, but reseeding will stop writing new hm.id panelist rows going forward.

## Twenty-fourth pass: VacancyCandidatesPage's Action column no longer renders a bare "--"

User flagged (via a screenshot of the HM's Vacancy Candidates list, plus a Kofi Mensah "already hired" Panel Feedback page as an example of what a resolved row looks like) that the Action column showed nothing but "--" for every row that wasn't actively awaiting a decision -- for a Hired candidate this read as broken/empty rather than "nothing left to do here."

- `VacancyCandidatesPage.tsx` had `VacancyCandidateRow`'s `stage`/`round`/`awaitingDecision` already available client-side -- no backend change needed. Added an `actionFor(r)` helper: `awaitingDecision` -> "Awaiting your decision" (unchanged); `HIRED`/`REJECTED` -> "Done"; no round yet (`APPLIED`) -> "Not yet interviewed"; otherwise (`SHORTLISTED`, mid-round, not yet ready) -> "Waiting on panel feedback". Every row now has a real word explaining its state instead of a dash.
- Checked whether `CandidateDecisionPage` (the "Panel Feedback - X" page reached by clicking a row) needed a matching change, since the user's message also described it -- confirmed it already does exactly what was asked: shows "This candidate has already been hired"/"already been rejected," still renders the full feedback history unconditionally, and the Hire/Reject/Proceed/Do Not Proceed buttons are already correctly absent (`awaitingDecision` gates that whole block). No change made there; the screenshot was illustrating the "Done" case's correct end state, not reporting a second bug.

**Verification:** frontend `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean (exit 0). No backend changes this pass.

## Twenty-fifth pass: Action column styling/ordering; hired/rejected candidates seeded with real feedback

- **Action column text now matches "Awaiting your decision"'s size/weight, colored by urgency.** "Done" and "Waiting on panel feedback" were plain `.vc-muted` (smaller, unstyled gray) from the Twenty-fourth pass. Added `.vc-action-done` (same 12px/600 as `.vc-awaiting`, green `#1e7a34`) and `.vc-action-waiting` (same size/weight, gray `var(--altrium-ink-soft)`) -- color is now the only thing that differs between the three states, not size.
- **Rows now sort by urgency:** awaiting-your-decision first, then still-waiting (panel feedback or not yet interviewed), done (hired/rejected) last. `actionFor()` returns a `priority` alongside its label/class; a `useMemo`'d stable sort orders `rows` by it before rendering, so ties keep the API's original order.
- **Root-caused and fixed "the panel feedback is not there" on an already-hired candidate's Panel Feedback page.** Archetypes 6 ("Already hired") and 7 ("Already rejected") in `seed-full-demo.ts` never had any interview/feedback records at all -- realistic recruiting doesn't hire or reject someone with zero recorded feedback. Both now walk real interview rounds: the hired archetype mirrors archetype 5's per-round shape ending in strong, unanimous final-round scores (interviewer/panelist2/mgmt when applicable); the rejected archetype gets one round of below-bar, mixed scores at the round they were rejected on. Also applied the same fix to the `allDecided` branch (Support Team Lead's 3 HIRE/REJECT/REJECT candidates), which had the identical gap.
- **Removed the "No panel feedback recorded yet." fallback from `CandidateDecisionPage.tsx`.** With hired/rejected candidates now always seeded with real feedback, this only fires for a genuinely feedback-less mid-round candidate -- and the warning banner already above it ("Waiting on the panel's feedback for X...") already covers that case on its own, so the line was redundant rather than informative.

**Verification:** frontend `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json` clean (exit 0) after the `VacancyCandidatesPage.tsx`/`.css` and `CandidateDecisionPage.tsx` edits. Backend (`seed-full-demo.ts`) hand-reviewed only -- same pre-existing sandbox limitation (backend `tsc`/`esbuild` unusable). User needs to rerun `npx tsx scripts/seed-full-demo.ts` from `backend/` to give already-hired/rejected candidates their new feedback history -- existing HIRED/REJECTED applications are found via find-or-create and won't be recreated, but the new `ensureInterviewAt` calls for them will still run and attach feedback that wasn't there before, since interview lookup is keyed by `(applicationId, vacancyStageId)`, not by whether the application itself is new.
